DESIGN.md

show
## Concept
The player pilots a lone dot moving through an endless scrolling tunnel. By tapping, they create a brief time‑slip that instantly jumps the dot forward a set distance while the tunnel continues to scroll, forcing strategic timing due to a cooldown.

## Player
A glowing dot representing the player’s presence in the tunnel. Its only controllable action is **time‑slip** (activate the forward jump). The dot otherwise drifts forward at the tunnel’s base speed.

## Core loop
Each frame the tunnel scrolls forward, presenting a stream of obstacles. The player watches the approaching patterns, decides whether to wait for a safe gap or trigger a time‑slip, and then taps to instantly advance the dot. After a slip the cooldown blocks further slips for a short interval, during which the player must rely on the tunnel’s base speed and any lateral dodges to survive. This cycle repeats continuously.

## Hazards / Obstacles
- **Static spikes:** Fixed points that protrude from the tunnel walls; colliding ends the run.
- **Sweeping blades:** Horizontal bars that move back and forth across the tunnel, their motion is fully telegraphed before sweeping.
- **Pulsing lasers:** Short bursts that fire at regular intervals, creating temporary gaps.
- **Rotating barriers:** Rings that rotate around the tunnel axis, the safe opening rotates and can be timed with a slip.
- **Moving shards:** Small projectiles that travel perpendicular to the tunnel flow, requiring precise slip timing to bypass.

## Score
- Each obstacle successfully passed without collision adds a point.
- Every successful time‑slip that clears at least one obstacle awards a bonus point.
- Collecting rare “chronon” pickups that appear in the tunnel grants extra points.

## Win and lose
The player loses when the dot contacts any hazard, triggering a Game Over overlay with the final score and restart prompt. The player wins by surviving until a predefined distance (or time) is reached, at which point a Victory screen celebrates the achievement and offers a restart.

## Feel
A high‑tempo, reflex‑driven experience with a neon‑glow aesthetic. The tunnel glows in rhythmic pulses, the dot flashes each time‑slip, and the soundtrack builds tension with sharp synth stabs. The pace accelerates as the run progresses, rewarding precise timing.

## Why it's fun
The thrill of pulling off a perfectly‑timed time‑slip to evade an imminent hazard makes the player feel a burst of control and satisfaction.

TASKS.md

show
1. Scaffold HTML & meta.json — create index.html with header (title, description, controls list), canvas, restart button, and empty script block; create meta.json matching header text and controls per CONVENTIONS.
2. Define core game state objects — add plain object literals for player, hazards, score, phase, and config at the top of the script with initial values.
3. Implement main loop — write startLoop() with cancelAnimationFrame discipline, requestAnimationFrame loop, try/catch body, calling update(dt) and render(), clearing canvas each frame.
4. Setup input handling — create a Set "keys", add keydown/keyup listeners, map R key and restart button to a shared reset() function.
5. Implement player physics and lateral control — dt‑scaled movement for left/right (or WASD), base forward drift, friction, bounds clamping, and ensure controls listed in meta.json are functional.
6. Add time‑slip mechanic — on tap (e.g., Space or mouse click) instantly advance the player forward by a fixed distance, start a cooldown timer preventing further slips until elapsed.
7. Create hazards system — define hazard data structures, spawn logic respecting SAFE_RADIUS to avoid immediate collisions, dt‑scaled motion, include at least one hazard type (spike).
8. Implement collision detection — check player vs hazards each frame, transition phase to "gameover" and stop active gameplay on hit.
9. Add scoring logic — increment score when player passes a hazard, award a bonus point on a successful time‑slip that clears at least one obstacle, draw score in top‑left corner.
10. Render scene — clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player (high‑contrast), and HUD each frame.
11. Display game‑over overlay — dim background, show "Game Over", final score, and "Press R or click Restart" instructions.
12. Expose observable contract — populate window.__game each frame with state, score, player {x, y, visible}, and a short objective sentence.
13. Integrate audio cues — lazy‑initialize AudioContext, implement beep() helper, play sounds on hit, score, and death events.

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Chrono Skip</title>
  <style>
    body { margin: 0; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e6e6f0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif; }
    .game-header { padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #1d1d2a; }
    .game-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; color: #7df9ff; }
    .game-header .game-description { margin: 4px 0; color: #a5a5b8; }
    .game-header .game-controls { margin: 4px 0; padding-left: 18px; color: #c8c8d8; }
    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #05050c; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }

<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Chrono Skip</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Tap to create a short time‑slip that instantly moves the dot forward while the tunnel continues scrolling, with a cooldown to force strategic timing.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Tap or click to time‑slip</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
    // === CONFIG =============================================================
    const W = 800, H = 600;
    const PLAYER_R = 8;
    const PLAYER_X = 200; // Fixed X screen position
    const VERT_SPEED = 300; // vertical speed (px/sec)
    const FORWARD_SPEED = 200; // world forward scroll speed (px/sec)
    const SLIP_DISTANCE = 150; // distance added on time‑slip
    const SLIP_COOLDOWN = 1.0; // seconds
    const HAZARD_W = 30, HAZARD_H = 60;
    const HAZARD_SPAWN_INTERVAL = 1.0; // seconds
    const SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN = 120;
    const SCORE_PER_HAZARD = 10;

    // === STATE ==============================================================
    const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

    let player = { y: H / 2, vy: 0 };
    let hazards = [];
    let score = 0;
    let highScore = 0;
    let phase = "menu"; // "menu" | "playing" | "gameover"
    let slipCooldown = 0;
    let slipRequested = false;
    let keys = new Set();
    let lastTime = performance.now();
    let loopId = null;
    let audioCtx = null;
    let distance = 0; // total forward distance traveled
    let spawnTimer = 0;

    function reset() {
      player.y = H / 2;
      player.vy = 0;
      hazards = [];
      score = 0;
      phase = "playing";
      slipCooldown = 0;
      slipRequested = false;
      keys.clear();
      distance = 0;
      spawnTimer = 0;
      lastTime = performance.now();
      publishState();
      startLoop();
    }

    // === LOOP / UPDATE =====================================================
    function startLoop() {
      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
      function tick(now) {
        try {
          const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
          lastTime = now;
          update(dt);
          render();
          publishState();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error("loop error", err);
        }
        loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }

    function spawnHazard() {
      const worldX = distance + W + Math.random() * 200;
      const y = HAZARD_H / 2 + Math.random() * (H - HAZARD_H);
      const playerWorldX = distance + PLAYER_X;
      const dx = worldX - playerWorldX;
      if (Math.abs(dx) < SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN) return;
      hazards.push({ worldX, y });
    }

    function update(dt) {
      if (phase !== "playing") return;
      distance += FORWARD_SPEED * dt;
      slipCooldown = Math.max(0, slipCooldown - dt);
      let move = 0;
      if (keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W")) move -= 1;
      if (keys.has("ArrowDown") || keys.has("s") || keys.has("S")) move += 1;
      player.vy = move * VERT_SPEED;
      player.y += player.vy * dt;
      if (player.y < PLAYER_R) player.y = PLAYER_R;
      if (player.y > H - PLAYER_R) player.y = H - PLAYER_R;
      if (slipRequested && slipCooldown <= 0) {
        distance += SLIP_DISTANCE;
        slipCooldown = SLIP_COOLDOWN;
        beep(800, 0.08);
        slipRequested = false;
      } else {
        slipRequested = false;
      }
      spawnTimer -= dt;
      while (spawnTimer <= 0) {
        spawnHazard();
        spawnTimer += HAZARD_SPAWN_INTERVAL;
      }
      for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const h = hazards[i];
        const screenX = h.worldX - distance;
        const dx = screenX - PLAYER_X;
        const dy = h.y - player.y;
        const rectHalfW = HAZARD_W / 2 + PLAYER_R;
        const rectHalfH = HAZARD_H / 2 + PLAYER_R;
        if (Math.abs(dx) < rectHalfW && Math.abs(dy) < rectHalfH) {
          phase = "gameover";
          if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
          beep(110, 0.3);
          break;
        }
        if (screenX + HAZARD_W / 2 < 0) {
          hazards.splice(i, 1);
          score += SCORE_PER_HAZARD;
          beep(440, 0.04);
        }
      }
    }

    // === RENDER =============================================================
    function render() {
      ctx.fillStyle = "#05050c";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ff5252";
      for (const h of hazards) {
        const screenX = h.worldX - distance;
        ctx.fillRect(screenX - HAZARD_W / 2, h.y - HAZARD_H / 2, HAZARD_W, HAZARD_H);
      }
      ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(PLAYER_X, player.y, PLAYER_R, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.textAlign = "left";
      ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
      if (phase === "gameover") {
        ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(5,5,12,0.65)";
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
        ctx.textAlign = "center";
        ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
        ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
        ctx.fillText("Game Over", W/2, H/2 - 20);
        ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
        ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
        ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, W/2, H/2 + 14);
        ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
        ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
        ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", W/2, H/2 + 42);
      }
    }

    // === INPUT ==============================================================
    document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
      if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
      if (ev.key === " " || ev.key === "Space") { slipRequested = true; ev.preventDefault(); }
      keys.add(ev.key);
    });
    document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
    restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);
    canvas.addEventListener("click", () => { slipRequested = true; });

    // === AUDIO (lazy) ======================================================
    function beep(freq, durSec) {
      try {
        if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
        const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
        osc.type = "triangle";
        osc.frequency.value = freq;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
        osc.start();
        osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      } catch {}
    }

    // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
    function publishState() {
      window.__game = {
        state: phase,
        score,
        player: { x: PLAYER_X, y: player.y, visible: player.y >= 0 && player.y < H },
        objective: "Advance the dot forward through a scrolling tunnel, using time‑slips strategically."
      };
    }

    // === BOOT ===============================================================
    function publishInitialState() {
      window.__game = {
        state: "menu",
        score: 0,
        player: { x: PLAYER_X, y: H/2, visible: true },
        objective: "Tap to create a time‑slip that moves the dot forward while the tunnel scrolls."
      };
    }
    publishInitialState();
    reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

## Concept
The player pilots a lone dot moving through an endless scrolling tunnel. By tapping, they create a brief time‑slip that instantly jumps the dot forward a set distance while the tunnel continues to scroll, forcing strategic timing due to a cooldown.

## Player
A glowing dot representing the player’s presence in the tunnel. Its only controllable action is **time‑slip** (activate the forward jump). The dot otherwise drifts forward at the tunnel’s base speed.

## Core loop
Each frame the tunnel scrolls forward, presenting a stream of obstacles. The player watches the approaching patterns, decides whether to wait for a safe gap or trigger a time‑slip, and then taps to instantly advance the dot. After a slip the cooldown blocks further slips for a short interval, during which the player must rely on the tunnel’s base speed and any lateral dodges to survive. This cycle repeats continuously.

## Hazards / Obstacles
- **Static spikes:** Fixed points that protrude from the tunnel walls; colliding ends the run.
- **Sweeping blades:** Horizontal bars that move back and forth across the tunnel, their motion is fully telegraphed before sweeping.
- **Pulsing lasers:** Short bursts that fire at regular intervals, creating temporary gaps.
- **Rotating barriers:** Rings that rotate around the tunnel axis, the safe opening rotates and can be timed with a slip.
- **Moving shards:** Small projectiles that travel perpendicular to the tunnel flow, requiring precise slip timing to bypass.

## Score
- Each obstacle successfully passed without collision adds a point.
- Every successful time‑slip that clears at least one obstacle awards a bonus point.
- Collecting rare “chronon” pickups that appear in the tunnel grants extra points.

## Win and lose
The player loses when the dot contacts any hazard, triggering a Game Over overlay with the final score and restart prompt. The player wins by surviving until a predefined distance (or time) is reached, at which point a Victory screen celebrates the achievement and offers a restart.

## Feel
A high‑tempo, reflex‑driven experience with a neon‑glow aesthetic. The tunnel glows in rhythmic pulses, the dot flashes each time‑slip, and the soundtrack builds tension with sharp synth stabs. The pace accelerates as the run progresses, rewarding precise timing.

## Why it's fun
The thrill of pulling off a perfectly‑timed time‑slip to evade an imminent hazard makes the player feel a burst of control and satisfaction.
```

Never make the player wait.\n92: 2. **Clear feedback** — the player always knows when something happened. Use color flashes, particles, brief screen shake, score popups, or sound on every meaningful event (hit, score, miss, death).\n93: 3. **Win/lose state** — the game has a clear end condition. On lose, draw \"Game Over\" + final score + restart instructions on the canvas. On win, celebrate (color burst, \"You Win!\" overlay).\n94: 4. **Restart without page reload** — both pressing R AND clicking the `<button id=\"restart\">` reset the game cleanly via the same code path. Mention both in `controls` (e.g. \"Press R or click Restart\"). On restart you MUST `cancelAnimationFrame` any pending loop before starting a new one (otherwise loops stack on each restart and the game eventually slows to a crawl).\n95: 5. **Progressive difficulty** — start gentle so the player can learn, then ramp up. Don't dump them into chaos at t=0.\n96: 6. **No dead time** — something interesting on screen at all times. Idle background animation (stars, gradient pulse, particles) is fine.\n97: 7. **Persistent score / progress UI** — drawn on the canvas at the top corner, always visible.\n98: 8. **Audio (optional but encouraged)** — generate tones with `OscillatorNode` (no external assets). **Do not auto-start audio on page load** — browsers block this and it will throw. Initialize the `AudioContext` lazily on the first keypress or click. Wrap audio code in try/catch.\n99: \n100: ## Playable Contract — non-negotiable\n101: \n102: These are not aspirations. A game that violates any of these is broken even if it runs without errors. After your implementation finishes, the validator loads the page in a real browser and runs an 8-second playtest with synthetic input — failures here are reported back to you and must be fixed before the game ships.\n103: \n104: ### 1. The player can lose\n105: \n106: There must be a collision check (or other end condition) that fires `gameOver` and stops the active gameplay loop. \"Game runs forever, no death\" is broken. Examples that have failed in the past: collision check compared a value against itself; \"spike\" collision only clamped position instead of killing the player; obstacles never reached the player.\n107: \n108: ### 2. The player can score\n109: \n110: The score must increase on a **discrete event** (a survived obstacle, a collected pickup, an enemy destroyed, a beat hit). \"Score = elapsed seconds\" is allowed but weak. Score must be drawn on the canvas at all times.\n111: \n112: ### 3. The first 3 seconds are survivable with no input\n113: \n114: Define a `SAFE_RADIUS` (e.g. 200px) around the player's starting position. When spawning hazards, **reject any spawn that is within `SAFE_RADIUS` of the player AND would reach them within 3 seconds at current hazard speed**. Pre-spawning hazards on top of the player at t=0 has caused frame-1 deaths in past games. Don't do it.\n115: \n116: ### 4. The control actually changes the outcome\n117: \n118: Whatever the player presses must, in the next frame, change a value that affects collision or score. If a control is purely cosmetic (e.g. \"stretch horizontally\" but the gaps are vertical), the game is fundamentally broken even if it runs. After writing, **re-read the game's concept and trace one frame**: does the input do what the concept claims?\n119: \n120: ### 5. dt-based physics, not per-frame constants\n121: \n122: All movement and timing must scale by delta time (seconds since last frame). Per-frame constants (`x += 5` every frame) make games run 2.4× faster on a 144Hz display.\n123: \n124: ```js\n125: let lastTime = performance.now();\n126: function loop(now) {\n127:   const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1); // seconds, clamped\n128:   lastTime = now;\n129:   // physics scaled by dt:\n130:   player.x += player.vx * dt;\n131:   enemy.y  += enemy.vy  * dt;\n132:   spawnTimer -= dt;\n133:   // ...\n134:   requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n135: }\n136: requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n137: ```\n138: \n139: ### 6. prompt (15,988 chars)
## Reference example: "asteroids"

The following is a complete, validator-passing game. **Use it as a structural template** — your output must follow the same section ordering, the same patterns for the loop / restart / observable contract, and the same overall code style. Do NOT reproduce this game; you are building a different game described in the prompt below. The example exists only to anchor structure.

Notice in particular:
- The `// === SECTION ===` divider banners and their fixed order: CONFIG → STATE → RESET → LOOP/UPDATE → RENDER → INPUT → AUDIO → OBSERVABLE CONTRACT → BOOT.
- Plain object literals for game state (no `class`).
- A `keys` Set polled each frame instead of mutating game state from key handlers.
- `if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` at the top of `startLoop()`.
- The animation loop body wrapped in `try/catch`.
- `publishState()` called once per frame at the end of the tick.
- `window.__game` populated honestly (player.visible reflects actual viewport position).
- AudioContext initialized lazily inside `beep()`, wrapped in try/catch.

### Example `meta.json`
```json
{
  "title": "Neon Asteroids",
  "description": "Rotate, thrust, and shoot. Big rocks split into small ones. Get hit and you're gone.",
  "controls": [
    "Left/Right (or A/D) to rotate",
    "Up (or W) to thrust",
    "Space to fire",
    "Press R or click Restart"
  ],
  "tags": ["arcade", "shooter", "physics"]
}
```

### Example `index.html`
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Neon Asteroids</title>
  <style>
    body { margin: 0; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e6e6f0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif; }
    .game-header { padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #1d1d2a; }
    .game-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; color: #7df9ff; }
    .game-header .game-description { margin: 4px 0; color: #a5a5b8; }
    .game-header .game-controls { margin: 4px 0; padding-left: 18px; color: #c8c8d8; }
    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #05050c; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Neon Asteroids</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Rotate, thrust, and shoot. Big rocks split into small ones. Get hit and you're gone.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Left/Right (or A/D) to rotate</li>
      <li>Up (or W) to thrust</li>
      <li>Space to fire</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  const W = 800, H = 600;
  const SHIP_RADIUS = 12;
  const SHIP_THRUST = 280;       // px/sec^2
  const SHIP_FRICTION = 0.6;     // velocity decay per second (1 = none)
  const SHIP_TURN = Math.PI * 1.8; // rad/sec
  const BULLET_SPEED = 520;      // px/sec
  const BULLET_LIFE = 1.0;       // sec
  const FIRE_COOLDOWN = 0.20;    // sec between shots
  const ASTEROID_BIG_R = 38;
  const ASTEROID_MID_R = 22;
  const ASTEROID_LIL_R = 12;
  const ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE = [40, 110];
  const SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN = 160; // no asteroid spawns inside this disk

  const SCORE_BIG = 20, SCORE_MID = 50, SCORE_LIL = 100;

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

  let ship;        // { x, y, vx, vy, angle, alive }
  let bullets;     // [{ x, y, vx, vy, life }]
  let asteroids;   // [{ x, y, vx, vy, r, shape: number[] }]
  let score;
  let highScore = 0;
  let phase;       // "playing" | "gameover"
  let fireCooldown;
  let keys;
  let lastTime;
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;

  function reset() {
    ship = { x: W / 2, y: H / 2, vx: 0, vy: 0, angle: -Math.PI / 2, alive: true };
    bullets = [];
    asteroids = [];
    spawnWave(5);
    score = 0;
    phase = "playing";
    fireCooldown = 0;
    keys = new Set();
    lastTime = performance.now();
    publishState();
    startLoop();
  }

  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      try {
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
        lastTime = now;
        update(dt);
        render();
        publishState();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("loop error", err);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  function spawnWave(n) {
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) spawnAsteroid(ASTEROID_BIG_R);
  }

  function spawnAsteroid(r) {
    let x, y, tries = 0;
    do {
      x = Math.random() * W;
      y = Math.random() * H;
      tries++;
    } while (Math.hypot(x - ship.x, y - ship.y) < SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN && tries < 30);
    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    const speed = ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0] + Math.random() * (ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[1] - ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0]);
    asteroids.push({
      x, y,
      vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
      vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
      r,
      shape: makeAsteroidShape(),
    });
  }

  function makeAsteroidShape() {
    // Per-vertex radius multipliers, 8-12 vertices for a knobbly silhouette.
    const n = 8 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 5);
    const out = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) out.push(0.75 + Math.random() * 0.45);
    return out;
  }

  // === UPDATE =============================================================
  function update(dt) {
    if (phase !== "playing") return;

    // Ship.
    if (ship.alive) {
      const turnIn = (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D") ? 1 : 0)
                   - (keys.has("ArrowLeft")  || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A") ? 1 : 0);
      ship.angle += turnIn * SHIP_TURN * dt;

      const thrusting = keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W");
      if (thrusting) {
        ship.vx += Math.cos(ship.angle) * SHIP_THRUST * dt;
        ship.vy += Math.sin(ship.angle) * SHIP_THRUST * dt;
      }
      // Mild friction so the ship doesn't drift forever.
      const decay = Math.pow(SHIP_FRICTION, dt);
      ship.vx *= decay;
      ship.vy *= decay;
      ship.x = wrap(ship.x + ship.vx * dt, W);
      ship.y = wrap(ship.y + ship.vy * dt, H);

      fireCooldown -= dt;
      if ((keys.has(" ") || keys.has("Space")) && fireCooldown <= 0) {
        fire();
        fireCooldown = FIRE_COOLDOWN;
      }
    }

    // Bullets.
    for (const b of bullets) {
      b.life -= dt;
      b.x = wrap(b.x + b.vx * dt, W);
      b.y = wrap(b.y + b.vy * dt, H);
    }
    bullets = bullets.filter((b) => b.life > 0);

    // Asteroids.
    for (const a of asteroids) {
      a.x = wrap(a.x + a.vx * dt, W);
      a.y = wrap(a.y + a.vy * dt, H);
    }

    // Bullet-asteroid collisions.
    for (let i = asteroids.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const a = asteroids[i];
      let hit = false;
      for (let j = bullets.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
        const b = bullets[j];
        if (Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y) < a.r) {
          bullets.splice(j, 1);
          hit = true;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (hit) splitAsteroid(i);
    }

    // Ship-asteroid collisions.
    if (ship.alive) {
      for (const a of asteroids) {
        if (Math.hypot(a.x - ship.x, a.y - ship.y) < a.r + SHIP_RADIUS * 0.6) {
          ship.alive = false;
          phase = "gameover";
          if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
          beep(110, 0.3);
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    // Wave clear -> spawn next.
    if (asteroids.length === 0 && phase === "playing") {
      spawnWave(5);
    }
  }

  function fire() {
    bullets.push({
      x: ship.x + Math.cos(ship.angle) * SHIP_RADIUS,
      y: ship.y + Math.sin(ship.angle) * SHIP_RADIUS,
      vx: ship.vx + Math.cos(ship.angle) * BULLET_SPEED,
      vy: ship.vy + Math.sin(ship.angle) * BULLET_SPEED,
      life: BULLET_LIFE,
    });
    beep(820, 0.04);
  }

  function splitAsteroid(idx) {
    const a = asteroids[idx];
    asteroids.splice(idx, 1);
    if (a.r === ASTEROID_BIG_R) {
      score += SCORE_BIG;
      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) addChild(a, ASTEROID_MID_R);
      beep(440, 0.06);
    } else if (a.r === ASTEROID_MID_R) {
      score += SCORE_MID;
      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) addChild(a, ASTEROID_LIL_R);
      beep(560, 0.05);
    } else {
      score += SCORE_LIL;
      beep(700, 0.05);
    }
  }

  function addChild(parent, r) {
    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    const speed = ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0] + Math.random() * (ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[1] - ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0]);
    asteroids.push({
      x: parent.x, y: parent.y,
      vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
      vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
      r,
      shape: makeAsteroidShape(),
    });
  }

  // === RENDER =============================================================
  function render() {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#05050c";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

    // Asteroids.
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#a5a5b8";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    for (const a of asteroids) drawAsteroid(a);

    // Bullets.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ffd23f";
    for (const b of bullets) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, 2.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }

    // Ship.
    if (ship.alive) drawShip();

    // HUD.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
    ctx.textAlign = "right";
    ctx.fillText(`Best ${highScore}`, W - 12, 22);

    if (phase === "gameover") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(5,5,12,0.65)";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
      ctx.fillText("Game Over", W / 2, H / 2 - 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, W / 2, H / 2 + 14);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", W / 2, H / 2 + 42);
    }
  }

  function drawShip() {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(ship.x, ship.y);
    ctx.rotate(ship.angle);
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#7df9ff";
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(SHIP_RADIUS, 0);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.9, SHIP_RADIUS * 0.7);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.5, 0);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.9, -SHIP_RADIUS * 0.7);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.stroke();
    if (keys && (keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W"))) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = "#ffd23f";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.5, 0);
      ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS - 6 - Math.random() * 4, 0);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function drawAsteroid(a) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (let i = 0; i < a.shape.length; i++) {
      const t = (i / a.shape.length) * Math.PI * 2;
      const r = a.r * a.shape[i];
      const x = a.x + Math.cos(t) * r;
      const y = a.y + Math.sin(t) * r;
      if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y); else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    }
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
    if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
    keys.add(ev.key);
    if (ev.key === " ") ev.preventDefault();
  });
  document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
  restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

  // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
  function beep(freq, durSec) {
    try {
      if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.type = "triangle";
      osc.frequency.value = freq;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    } catch {}
  }

  // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
  function publishState() {
    window.__game = {
      state: phase,                                  // "playing" | "gameover"
      score,
      player: {
        x: ship.x,
        y: ship.y,
        // wrap() keeps coords in [0, W) and [0, H), so visible is always true
        // — but assert it explicitly so the contract is honest.
        visible: ship.x >= 0 && ship.x < W && ship.y >= 0 && ship.y < H,
      },
      objective: "Shoot the asteroids before they hit your ship. Big rocks split into smaller ones.",
    };
  }

  // === HELPERS ============================================================
  function wrap(v, max) {
    if (v < 0) return v + max;
    if (v >= max) return v - max;
    return v;
  }

  // === BOOT ===============================================================
  reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

Don't dump them into chaos at t=0.\n96: 6. **No dead time** — something interesting on screen at all times. Idle background animation (stars, gradient pulse, particles) is fine.\n97: 7. **Persistent score / progress UI** — drawn on the canvas at the top corner, always visible.\n98: 8. **Audio (optional but encouraged)** — generate tones with `OscillatorNode` (no external assets). **Do not auto-start audio on page load** — browsers block this and it will throw. After your implementation finishes, the validator loads the page in a real browser and runs an 8-second playtest with synthetic input — failures here are reported back to you and must be fixed before the game ships.\n103: \n104: ### 1. The player can lose\n105: \n106: There must be a collision check (or other end condition) that fires `gameOver` and stops the active gameplay loop. \"Game runs forever, no death\" is broken. Examples that have failed in the past: collision check compared a value against itself; \"spike\" collision only clamped position instead of killing the player; obstacles never reached the player.\n107: \n108: ### 2. The player can score\n109: \n110: The score must increase on a **discrete event** (a survived obstacle, a collected pickup, an enemy destroyed, a beat hit). \"Score = elapsed seconds\" is allowed but weak. Pre-spawning hazards on top of the player at t=0 has caused frame-1 deaths in past games. Don't do it.\n115: \n116: ### 4. The control actually changes the outcome\n117: \n118: Whatever the player presses must, in the next frame, change a value that affects collision or score. If a control is purely cosmetic (e.g. \"stretch horizontally\" but the gaps are vertical), the game is fundamentally broken even if it runs. Per-frame constants (`x += 5` every frame) make games run 2.4× faster on a 144Hz display.\n123: \n124: ```js\n125: let lastTime = performance.now();\n126: function loop(now) {\n127:   const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1); // seconds, clamped\n128:   lastTime = now;\n129:   // physics scaled by dt:\n130:   player.x += player.vx * dt;\n131:   enemy.y  += enemy.vy  * dt;\n132:   spawnTimer -= dt;\n133:   // ...\n134:   requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n135: }\n136: requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n137: ```\n138: \n139: ### 6. The simplest approach is to update them at the end of your render/update step each frame.\n176: - `state` must only ever be one of the three exact strings. No other values.\n177: - `player.visible` is critical. **If your camera follows the player, the camera math must keep `player.visible` = true at all times.** A camera that lags the player off-screen is the most common failure here.\n178: - `objective` is one short sentence written for a first-time player: \"what am I trying to do?\" — derivable from observing the game alone, not from reading the meta.\n179: \n180: If your game has multiple \"players\" or no clear single player, expose the position of the most player-like entity (the thing the human controls).\n181: \n182: ## What the validator checks\n183: \n184: Your output is checked programmatically. Add reset function:

```js
function reset() {
  // reset state
  player = { x: CONFIG.W/2, y: CONFIG.H/2, vx: 0, vy: 0, alive: true };
  hazards = [];
  score = 0;
  phase = "playing";
  keys.clear();
  lastTime = performance.now();
  startLoop();
}
```

But startLoop defined later.

Add startLoop:

```js
function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      // placeholder update and render
      // For now, just clear canvas
      ctx.clearRect(0,0,CONFIG.W,CONFIG.H);
    } catch(e) {
      console.error("loop error", e);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}
```

We need `ctx` variable: we must get canvas and context.

Add code:

```js
const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
```

Add input handling:

```js
document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
  if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") {
    reset();
  } else {
    keys.add(ev.key);
  }
});
document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => {
  keys.delete(ev.key);
});
document.getElementById("restart").addEventListener("click", reset);
```

// === CONFIG =============================================================
const W = 800, H = 600;
const PLAYER_R = 12;
const PLAYER_SPEED = 250; // px/s
const PLAYER_FRICTION = 0.9; // per second friction factor
const SLIP_DISTANCE = 120;
const SLIP_COOLDOWN = 1.0; // sec
const HAZARD_R = 14;
const HAZARD_SPEED = 180; // px/s leftwards
const SPAWN_INTERVAL = 2.0; // sec
const SAFE_RADIUS = 150; // for spawn safety
```

Define references and variables:

// === STATE ==============================================================
const canvas = document.getElementById('game');
const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
const restartBtn = document.getElementById('restart');

let player;
let hazards;
let score;
let highScore = 0;
let phase; // "menu" | "playing" | "gameover"
let slipCooldown;
let spawnTimer;
let keys;
let lastTime;
let loopId = null;
let audioCtx = null;
```

Reset function:

```js
// === RESET ==============================================================
function reset() {
  player = { x: W * 0.2, y: H / 2, vx: 0, vy: 0, alive: true };
  hazards = [];
  score = 0;
  phase = 'playing';
  slipCooldown = 0;
  spawnTimer = 0;
  keys = new Set();
  lastTime = performance.now();
  publishState();
  startLoop();
}
```

Loop start:

```js
// === LOOP ==============================================================
function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      update(dt);
      render();
      publishState();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('loop error', err);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}
```

Update:

```js
// === UPDATE =============================================================
function update(dt) {
  if (phase !== 'playing') return;

  // Player control
  let ax = 0, ay = 0;
  if (keys.has('ArrowLeft') || keys.has('a') || keys.has('A')) ax -= 1;
  if (keys.has('ArrowRight') || keys.has('d') || keys.has('D')) ax += 1;
  if (keys.has('ArrowUp') || keys.has('w') || keys.has('W')) ay -= 1;
  if (keys.has('ArrowDown') || keys.has('s') || keys.has('S')) ay += 1;
  // Normalize diagonal speed
  if (ax !== 0 && ay !== 0) {
    const inv = 1 / Math.sqrt(2);
    ax *= inv;
    ay *= inv;
  }
  player.vx = ax * PLAYER_SPEED;
  player.vy = ay * PLAYER_SPEED;

  // Apply friction (optional)
  player.vx *= Math.pow(PLAYER_FRICTION, dt);
  player.vy *= Math.pow(PLAYER_FRICTION, dt);

  // Update position
  player.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(W, player.x + player.vx * dt));
  player.y = Math.max(0, Math.min(H, player.y + player.vy * dt));

  // Slip cooldown timer
  slipCooldown = Math.max(0, slipCooldown - dt);

  // Hazards movement and removal
  for (const h of hazards) {
    h.x += h.vx * dt;
    h.y += h.vy * dt;
  }
  hazards = hazards.filter(h => h.x + HAZARD_R > 0 && h.y + HAZARD_R > 0 && h.x - HAZARD_R < W && h.y - HAZARD_R < H);

  // Spawn logic
  spawnTimer -= dt;
  if (spawnTimer <= 0) {
    spawnHazard();
    spawnTimer = SPAWN_INTERVAL;
  }

  // Collision detection (task 8)
  if (player.alive) {
    for (const h of hazards) {
      const dist = Math.hypot(player.x - h.x, player.y - h.y);
      if (dist < PLAYER_R + HAZARD_R) {
        player.alive = false;
        phase = 'gameover';
        if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
        beep(110, 0.3);
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  // Score maybe (increment over time)
  score = Math.floor(score + dt * 10); // just for test
}
```

But we need function spawnHazard defined (task 7) and spike representation.

We can write for hazards: each hazard object: { x, y, vx: -HAZARD_SPEED, vy: 0, r: HAZARD_R }.

Spawn hazard:

```js
function spawnHazard() {
  // spawn at right edge
  let x = W + HAZARD_R;
  let y = HAZARD_R + Math.random() * (H - 2 * HAZARD_R);
  // ensure safe distance from player at spawn
  const maxTries = 30;
  let tries = 0;
  while (Math.hypot(x - player.x, y - player.y) < SAFE_RADIUS && tries < maxTries) {
    y = HAZARD_R + Math.random() * (H - 2 * HAZARD_R);
    tries++;
  }
  hazards.push({ x, y, vx: -HAZARD_SPEED, vy: 0, r: HAZARD_R });
}
```

Task 7 also suggested shape for spike; can be drawn as a simple triangle; we can implement render.

Render:

```js
// === RENDER =============================================================
function render() {
  ctx.fillStyle = '#05050c';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

  // Draw hazards as spikes (simple triangle)
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ff5252';
  for (const h of hazards) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(h.x, h.y - h.r);
    ctx.lineTo(h.x - h.r, h.y + h.r);
    ctx.lineTo(h.x + h.r, h.y + h.r);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fill();
  }

  // Draw player dot
  if (player.alive) {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#7df9ff';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, PLAYER_R, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }

  // HUD - score
  ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e6f0';
  ctx.font = '16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);

  if (phase === 'gameover') {
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(5,5,12,0.65)';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.font = '44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ff52ae';
    ctx.fillText('Game Over', W / 2, H / 2 - 20);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e6f0';
    ctx.font = '20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
    ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, W / 2, H / 2 + 14);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#a5a5b8';
    ctx.font = '16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
    ctx.fillText('Press R or click Restart', W / 2, H / 2 + 42);
  }
}
```

Input handling:

```js
// === INPUT ==============================================================
document.addEventListener('keydown', (ev) => {
  if (ev.key === 'r' || ev.key === 'R') {
    reset();
    return;
  }
  keys.add(ev.key);
  // Slip activation on Space or click is handled separately.
  if (ev.key === ' ') ev.preventDefault();
});

document.addEventListener('keyup', (ev) => {
  keys.delete(ev.key);
});

canvas.addEventListener('click', (ev) => {
  attemptSlip();
});

document.addEventListener('keydown', (ev) => {
  if (ev.key === ' ') {
    attemptSlip();
    ev.preventDefault();
  }
});

restartBtn.addEventListener('click', reset);

Define attemptSlip function:

```js
function attemptSlip() {
  if (slipCooldown <= 0 && player.alive && phase === 'playing') {
    player.x = Math.min(W, player.x + SLIP_DISTANCE);
    slipCooldown = SLIP_COOLDOWN;
    beep(820, 0.04);
  }
}
```

Add audio:

```js
// === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
function beep(freq, durSec) {
  try {
    if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
    const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
    osc.type = 'triangle';
    osc.frequency.value = freq;
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
  } catch (e) {
    // ignore audio errors
  }
}
```

Now observable contract:

```js
// === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ================================================
function publishState() {
  window.__game = {
    state: phase,
    score: score,
    player: {
      x: player.x,
      y: player.y,
      visible: player.x >= 0 && player.x < W && player.y >= 0 && player.y < H,
    },
    objective: 'Avoid spikes by moving and using time‑slip to jump forward.',
  };
}

We need to ensure keys set defined before being used (in input listeners we need keys defined; but reset defines keys, and we call reset in BOOT after the script loads but before input listeners are attached? Actually input listeners are added before reset, but keys is defined as a variable (let keys) but is undefined until reset. In the keydown handler, we call keys.add. If keys is undefined we get error. To avoid that, we should initialize keys as new Set() early; i.e., in STATE we can set keys = new Set(); but reset also sets it. In input listener, we can guard: if (!keys) keys = new Set(); but better to initialize earlier. So in STATE: let keys = new Set(); So we can have let keys; then later in reset set to new Set. However in keydown listeners that are attached before reset, keys will be undefined if the user triggers before reset (unlikely as script loads and reset is called soon after script start). But we can set keys = new Set() at variable definition to guarantee it exists. Then reset can set new Set again.

Thus define `let keys = new Set();` in STATE.

Similarly, other variables: player etc null initially but they will be defined in reset.

Make sure player object exist before input listeners that reference player? The attemptSlip accesses player, also defined after reset. Input listener may be triggered before reset only if user quickly interacts before script loads; not a concern.

Now we need to ensure that we didn't forget any referenced functions: attemptSlip defined before UI events? We'll define attemptSlip function before attaching events or after; functions are hoisted (function declarations) so fine.

lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);\n      lastTime = now;\n      update(dt);\n      render();\n      publishState();\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error('loop error', err);\n    }\n    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n  }\n  loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n}\n```\n\nUpdate:\n\n```js\n// === UPDATE =============================================================\nfunction update(dt) {\n  if (phase !== 'playing') return;\n\n  // Player control\n  let ax = 0, ay = 0;\n  if (keys.has('ArrowLeft') || keys.has('a') || keys.has('A')) ax -= 1;\n  if (keys.has('ArrowRight') || keys.has('d') || keys.has('D')) ax += 1;\n  if (keys.has('ArrowUp') || keys.has('w') || keys.has('W')) ay -= 1;\n  if (keys.has('ArrowDown') || keys.has('s') || keys.has('S')) ay += 1;\n  // Normalize diagonal speed\n  if (ax !== 0 && ay !== 0) {\n    const inv = 1 / Math.sqrt(2);\n    ax *= inv;\n    ay *= inv;\n  }\n  player.vx = ax * PLAYER_SPEED;\n  player.vy = ay * PLAYER_SPEED;\n\n  // Apply friction (optional)\n  player.vx *= Math.pow(PLAYER_FRICTION, dt);\n  player.vy *= Math.pow(PLAYER_FRICTION, dt);\n\n  // Update position\n  player.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(W, player.x + player.vx * dt));\n  player.y = Math.max(0, Math.min(H, player.y + player.vy * dt));\n\n  // Slip cooldown timer\n  slipCooldown = Math.max(0, slipCooldown - dt);\n\n  // Hazards movement and removal\n  for (const h of hazards) {\n    h.x += h.vx * dt;\n    h.y += h.vy * dt;\n  }\n  hazards = hazards.filter(h => h.x + HAZARD_R > 0 && h.y + HAZARD_R > 0 && h.x - 2 * HAZARD_R);\n  // ensure safe distance from player at spawn\n  const maxTries = 30;\n  let tries = 0;\n  while (Math.hypot(x - player.x, y - player.y) < SAFE_RADIUS && tries < maxTries) {\n    y = HAZARD_R + Math.random() * (H - 2 * HAZARD_R);\n    tries++;\n  }\n  hazards.push({ x, y, vx: -HAZARD_SPEED, vy: 0, r: HAZARD_R });\n}\n```\n\nTask 7 also suggested shape for spike; can be drawn as a simple triangle; we can implement render.\n\nRender:\n\n```js\n// === RENDER =============================================================\nfunction render() {\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#05050c';\n  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);\n\n  // Draw hazards as spikes (simple triangle)\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#ff5252';\n  for (const h of hazards) {\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.moveTo(h.x, h.y - h.r);\n    ctx.lineTo(h.x - h.r, h.y + h.r);\n    ctx.lineTo(h.x + h.r, h.y + h.r);\n    ctx.closePath();\n    ctx.fill();\n  }\n\n  // Draw player dot\n  if (player.alive) {\n    ctx.fillStyle = '#7df9ff';\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, PLAYER_R, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n    ctx.fill();\n  }\n\n  // HUD - score\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e6f0';\n  ctx.font = '16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';\n  ctx.textAlign = 'left';\n  ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);\n\n  if (phase === 'gameover') {\n    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(5,5,12,0.65)';\n    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);\n    ctx.textAlign = 'center';\n    ctx.font = '44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';\n    ctx.fillStyle = '#ff52ae';\n    ctx.fillText('Game Over', W / 2, H / 2 - 20);\n    ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e6f0';\n    ctx.font = '20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';\n    ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, W / 2, H / 2 + 14);\n    ctx.fillStyle = '#a5a5b8';\n    ctx.font = '16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';\n    ctx.fillText('Press R or click Restart', W / 2, H / 2 + 42);\n  }\n}\n```\n\nInput handling:\n\n```js\n// === INPUT ==============================================================\ndocument.addEventListener('keydown', (ev) => {\n  if (ev.key === 'r' || ev.key === 'R') {\n    reset();\n    return;\n  }\n  keys.add(ev.key);\n  // Slip activation on Space or click is handled separately.\n  if (ev.key === ' ') ev.preventDefault();\n});\n\ndocument.addEventListener('keyup', (ev) => {\n  keys.delete(ev.key);\n});\n\ncanvas.addEventListener('click', (ev) => {\n  attemptSlip();\n});\n\ndocument.addEventListener('keydown', (ev) => {\n  if (ev.key === ' ') {\n    attemptSlip();\n    ev.preventDefault();\n  }\n});\n\nrestartBtn.addEventListener('click', reset);\n```\n\nBut we have duplicate keydown listeners. We'll combine or separate but ok.\n\nDefine attemptSlip function:\n\n```js\nfunction attemptSlip() {\n  if (slipCooldown <= 0 && player.alive && phase === 'playing') {\n    player.x = Math.min(W, player.x + SLIP_DISTANCE);\n    slipCooldown = SLIP_COOLDOWN;\n    beep(820, 0.04);\n  }\n}\n```\n\nAdd audio:\n\n```js\n// === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================\nfunction beep(freq, durSec) {\n  try {\n    if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();\n    const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();\n    const gain = audioCtx.createGain();\n    osc.type = 'triangle';\n    osc.frequency.value = freq;\n    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);\n    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);\n    osc.connect(gain);\n    gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);\n    osc.start();\n    osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);\n  } catch (e) {\n    // ignore audio errors\n  }\n}\n```\n\nNow observable contract:\n\n```js\n// === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ================================================\nfunction publishState() {\n  window.__game = {\n    state: phase,\n    score: score,\n    player: {\n      x: player.x,\n      y: player.y,\n      visible: player.x >= 0 && player.x < W && player.y >= 0 && player.y < H,\n    },\n    objective: 'Avoid spikes by moving and using time‑slip to jump forward.',\n  };\n}\n```\n\nHelpers: optionally the wrap function not needed.\n\nBoot:\n\n```js\n// === BOOT ===============================================================\nreset();\n```\n\nNow compile script.\n\nWe need to ensure keys set defined before being used (in input listeners we need keys defined; but reset defines keys, and we call reset in BOOT after the script loads but before input listeners are attached? Let's create the content:

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Chrono Skip</title>
  <style>
    body { margin: 0; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e6e6f0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif; }
    .game-header { padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #1d1d2a; }
    .game-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; color: #7df9ff; }
    .game-header .game-description { margin: 4px 0; color: #a5a5b8; }
    .game-header .game-controls { margin: 4px 0; padding-left: 18px; color: #c8c8d8; }
    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #05050c; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Chrono Skip</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Tap to create a short time‑slip that instantly moves the dot forward while the tunnel continues scrolling, with a cooldown to force strategic timing.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Tap or click to time‑slip</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
    // === CONFIG =============================================================
    const W = 800, H = 600;
    const PLAYER_R = 8;
    const PLAYER_X = 200; // Fixed X screen position
    const VERT_SPEED = 300; // vertical speed (px/sec)
    const FORWARD_SPEED = 200; // world forward scroll speed (px/sec)
    const SLIP_DISTANCE = 150; // distance added on time‑slip
    const SLIP_COOLDOWN = 1.0; // seconds
    const HAZARD_W = 30, HAZARD_H = 60;
    const HAZARD_SPAWN_INTERVAL = 1.0; // seconds
    const SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN = 120;
    const SCORE_PER_HAZARD = 10;

    // === STATE ==============================================================
    const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

    let player = { y: H / 2, vy: 0 };
    let hazards = [];
    let score = 0;
    let highScore = 0;
    let phase = "menu"; // "menu" | "playing" | "gameover"
    let slipCooldown = 0;
    let slipRequested = false;
    let keys = new Set();
    let lastTime = performance.now();
    let loopId = null;
    let audioCtx = null;
    let distance = 0; // total forward distance traveled
    let spawnTimer = 0;

    function reset() {
      player.y = H / 2;
      player.vy = 0;
      hazards = [];
      score = 0;
      phase = "playing";
      slipCooldown = 0;
      slipRequested = false;
      keys.clear();
      distance = 0;
      spawnTimer = 0;
      lastTime = performance.now();
      publishState();
      startLoop();
    }

    // === LOOP / UPDATE =====================================================
    function startLoop() {
      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
      function tick(now) {
        try {
          const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
          lastTime = now;
          update(dt);
          render();
          publishState();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error("loop error", err);
        }
        loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }

    function spawnHazard() {
      // World X coordinate ahead of current distance
      const worldX = distance + W + Math.random() * 200;
      const y = HAZARD_H / 2 + Math.random() * (H - HAZARD_H);
      // Ensure safe spawn distance from player
      const playerWorldX = distance + PLAYER_X;
      const dx = worldX - playerWorldX;
      if (Math.abs(dx) < SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN) return;
      hazards.push({ worldX, y });
    }

    function update(dt) {
      if (phase !== "playing") return;
      // Move forward
      distance += FORWARD_SPEED * dt;
      // Cooldowns
      slipCooldown = Math.max(0, slipCooldown - dt);
      // Input: vertical movement
      let move = 0;
      if (keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W")) move -= 1;
      if (keys.has("ArrowDown") || keys.has("s") || keys.has("S")) move += 1;
      player.vy = move * VERT_SPEED;
      player.y += player.vy * dt;
      // Clamp vertical position
      if (player.y < PLAYER_R) player.y = PLAYER_R;
      if (player.y > H - PLAYER_R) player.y = H - PLAYER_R;
      // Time‑slip handling (Space bar or mouse click)
      if (slipRequested && slipCooldown <= 0) {
        distance += SLIP_DISTANCE;
        slipCooldown = SLIP_COOLDOWN;
        beep(800, 0.08);
        slipRequested = false;
      } else {
        slipRequested = false;
      }
      // Spawn hazards
      spawnTimer -= dt;
      while (spawnTimer <= 0) {
        spawnHazard();
        spawnTimer += HAZARD_SPAWN_INTERVAL;
      }
      // Update hazards and check collisions / scoring
      for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const h = hazards[i];
        const screenX = h.worldX - distance;
        // Collision detection (circle vs rect approx)
        const dx = screenX - PLAYER_X;
        const dy = h.y - player.y;
        const rectHalfW = HAZARD_W / 2 + PLAYER_R;
        const rectHalfH = HAZARD_H / 2 + PLAYER_R;
        if (Math.abs(dx) < rectHalfW && Math.abs(dy) < rectHalfH) {
          phase = "gameover";
          if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
          beep(110, 0.3);
          break;
        }
        // Passed left side -> score
        if (screenX + HAZARD_W / 2 < 0) {
          hazards.splice(i, 1);
          score += SCORE_PER_HAZARD;
          beep(440, 0.04);
        }
      }
    }

    // === RENDER =============================================================
    function render() {
      // Clear background
      ctx.fillStyle = "#05050c";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      // Draw hazards
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ff5252";
      for (const h of hazards) {
        const screenX = h.worldX - distance;
        ctx.fillRect(screenX - HAZARD_W / 2, h.y - HAZARD_H / 2, HAZARD_W, HAZARD_H);
      }
      // Draw player
      ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(PLAYER_X, player.y, PLAYER_R, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      // HUD – score
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.textAlign = "left";
      ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
      // Game over overlay
      if (phase === "gameover") {
        ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(5,5,12,0.65)";
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
        ctx.textAlign = "center";
        ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
        ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
        ctx.fillText("Game Over", W/2, H/2 - 20);
        ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
        ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
        ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, W/2, H/2 + 14);
        ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
        ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
        ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", W/2, H/2 + 42);
      }
    }

    // === INPUT ==============================================================
    document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
      if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
      if (ev.key === " " || ev.key === "Space") { slipRequested = true; ev.preventDefault(); }
      keys.add(ev.key);
    });
    document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
    restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);
    canvas.addEventListener("click", () => { slipRequested = true; });

    // === AUDIO (lazy) ======================================================
    function beep(freq, durSec) {
      try {
        if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
        const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
        osc.type = "triangle";
        osc.frequency.value = freq;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
        osc.start();
        osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      } catch {}
    }

    // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
    function publishState() {
      window.__game = {
        state: phase,
        score,
        player: { x: PLAYER_X, y: player.y, visible: player.y >= 0 && player.y < H },
        objective: "Advance the dot forward through a scrolling tunnel, using time‑slips strategically."
      };
    }

    // === BOOT ===============================================================
    // Initial observable state before the game starts
    function publishInitialState() {
      window.__game = {
        state: "menu",
        score: 0,
        player: { x: PLAYER_X, y: H/2, visible: true },
        objective: "Tap to create a time‑slip that moves the dot forward while the tunnel scrolls."
      };
    }
    publishInitialState();
    reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

Check for any syntax errors: Need to ensure we close all parentheses and curly braces. Looks fine.

prompt (5,127 chars)
Read CONVENTIONS.md and DESIGN.md in your working directory before doing anything else.

Your job in this stage is to break the implementation into a numbered list of small, ordered tasks. The next stage will work through this list one task at a time, in a single LLM session, reloading the page after each task and capturing console errors for you to fix.

Title: Chrono Skip
Concept: Tap to create a short time‑slip that instantly moves the dot forward a fixed distance while the tunnel continues scrolling, with a cooldown to force strategic timing.

**You must produce one output file: `TASKS.md` in the working directory.** Use your write/edit tool to create this file on disk. After your turn ends, the validator runs `readFile("TASKS.md")` — if the file does not exist on disk, validation fails and your work is wasted. **Do NOT reply with the task list as inline chat text.** The list must be written to the file.

**Tool calls MUST be emitted as actual function/tool invocations — NOT described or simulated inside your reasoning/analysis text.** Writing tool-shaped JSON like `{"filePath":"TASKS.md","content":"..."}` *inside your chain-of-thought* does NOT invoke any tool. It is just text in the analysis channel that nothing reads or executes. The same goes for `{"pattern":"**/*"}` etc. — those only do something when emitted as a real `tool_use` event from the action channel, never when written into your thinking. Several prior runs have failed by hallucinating tool calls inside reasoning; do not repeat that pattern. If at any point you "describe" a tool call in your thinking instead of invoking it, you must immediately invoke the real tool afterward. The format MUST be a numbered list, one task per line, each line starting with `<number>.`:

```
1. <Task title> — <one-sentence description of what to do>
2. <Task title> — <one-sentence description>
...
```

The next stage parses tasks by matching `^\d+\.` at the start of each line. Don't use bullet sub-lists, don't number sub-tasks (1.1, 1.2). One number, one task, one line.

### What makes a good task

- **Small enough** to complete in a single coding turn (~50–150 lines of edits).
- **Testable**: after this task, what does the page do that it didn't before?
- **Leaves the file runnable**: at the end of the task, no syntax errors, no uncaught exceptions on load. Even if the game isn't playable yet, it must load.
- **Specific**: don't say "implement player". Say "implement player movement: arrow keys set keys.left/right; in update(), apply acceleration in the pressed direction with cap at MAX_SPEED, friction otherwise; clamp x to canvas bounds."
- **Concrete numbers**: this is where pixel sizes, ms intervals, and px/sec speeds live. Pick them. The implement stage will use them verbatim.

### Required coverage (in roughly this order)

Cover at minimum, ordered as below. Add tasks if the design demands more (powerups, level progression, screen wrap, etc):

1. **HTML scaffold + meta.json**: header (h1, description, controls list), canvas, restart button per CONVENTIONS.md. Stub `<script>` block (empty). meta.json matching header text.
2. **Game state objects**: player, hazards, score, phase using plain object literals at the top of the script. Set initial values.
3. **requestAnimationFrame loop**: startLoop() with `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` discipline at the top. Body wrapped in try/catch. Calls update(dt) and render(). Don't draw anything yet besides clearing the canvas.
4. **Input**: a `keys` Set populated by document keydown/keyup. R-key restart hook. Restart button click hook. Both call the same reset() function.
5. **Player movement**: dt-based physics tied to the key inputs from task 4. Verify each control listed in meta.json actually changes a value.
6. **Hazards**: data structure, spawn rules with a SAFE_RADIUS so nothing spawns on top of the player at t=0, motion (dt-scaled).
7. **Collision detection**: player vs hazards. On hit, transition phase to "gameover" and stop the active gameplay.
8. **Score**: discrete events that increase score, drawn on canvas at top-left.
9. **Render**: clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player, HUD. Player must be the highest-contrast object on screen.
10. **Game-over overlay**: dim background, "Game Over", final score, "Press R or click Restart".
11. **Observable contract**: populate `window.__game = { state, score, player:{x,y,visible}, objective }` every frame. `state` is "playing" | "gameover". `objective` is a 10–200 char single-sentence description.
12. **Audio**: lazy-init AudioContext inside a beep() helper, wrapped in try/catch. Hook into hit / score / death events.

You may merge two trivial tasks if they truly belong together (e.g. tasks 1+2 if state is just three lines). You may split a complex task in two. Aim for 10–15 tasks total.

**Final check before you stop**: confirm you actually called your write/edit tool with `TASKS.md` as the path. If you only described the task list in chat without invoking the tool, the file will not exist on disk and validation will fail. After the file is written, stop. Do not begin implementation.
